# Six-case populated human review queue

AI recommendations only. Every human decision remains pending.
Markdown trims line-end whitespace for display. Exact excerpt characters are preserved in HUMAN_REVIEW_QUEUE.json and selected.json.

## EIV2_FV_001-C001

Case/category: EIV2_FV_001 / factual_verification

Type: claim_segmentation; flags:

Exact report/unit text:

OpenAI 的隐私政策不适用于其代表商业产品客户\(例如 API 服务客户\)处理的内容

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: factual

分开政策适用范围与客户协议约束两个可核查断言。

Uncertainty: 预标注需人工确认，尚非 gold。

Parent report sentence: OpenAI 的隐私政策不适用于其代表商业产品客户\(例如 API 服务客户\)处理的内容;此类数据的使用受相关客户协议约束。

Evidence: ev_6f1b032c397627b6 — https://openai.com/zh-Hans-CN/policies/row-privacy-policy

```text
隐私政策 | OpenAI
OpenAI
输入语言
…
Updated: 2026年2月6日
隐私政策
（
先前版本
⁠
）
如果您位于欧洲经济区、英国或瑞士，可以阅读
此版本
的隐私政策。如果您位于美国，可以阅读
此版本
的隐私政策。
如果您位于韩国，请参阅
韩国附录
⁠
。
在 OpenAI，我们的使命是确保通用人工智能惠及全人类。我们打造了 ChatGPT 和 Sora 等工具，旨在帮助人们学习、创作并解决问题。OpenAI（连同我们的关联公司，统称为“OpenAI”、“我们”或“我方”）重视您的隐私，并坚定致力于保障我们从您处获得或与您相关的任何信息的安全。本隐私政策阐明了当您使用我们的网站、应用程序和服务（统称为“服务”）时，我们如何收集与您相关的个人数据以及如何使用这些数据。
本隐私政策不适用于我们代表商业产品客户（例如我们的 API 服务客户）处理的内容。我们对该数据的使用受相关客户协议约束，此类协议涵盖了对相关产品的访问及使用规则。
有关我们如何收集和使用训练信息以开发驱动 ChatGPT 及其他服务的语言模型，以及您在这些信息方面可以作出的选择，请参阅本政策以及
这篇帮助中心文章
⁠
（在新窗口中打开）
。
1. 我们收集的个人数据
我们收集与您相关的个人数据（“个人数据”）如下：
您提供的个人数据：
我们会在您创建帐户以使用我们的服务或与我们沟通时收集个人数据，具体如下：
账户信息
：当您创建账户时，我们会收集与您的账户相关的信息，包括您的姓名、联系信息、账户凭据、出生日期、支付信息和交易历史记录（统称为“账户信息”）。我们的一些服务还允许您上传个人资料照片、用户名或其他信息，作为账户信息的一部分。
用户内容：
我们会收集您在向我们的服务提供输入时提交的个人数据（“内容”），包括您的提示以及您上传的其他内容，例如
文件
⁠
（在新窗口中打开）
、
图片
⁠
（在新窗口中打开）
、
音频和视频
⁠
（在新窗口中打开）
、
Sora 角色
⁠
（在新窗口中打开）
，以及来自
连接服务
⁠
（在新窗口中打开）
的数据，具体取决于您使用的功能。我们的一些服务允许您与其他用户互动，例如发帖、评论或发送消息，我们也将这些互动视为内容的一部分。
```

## EIV2_FV_001-C003

Case/category: EIV2_FV_001 / factual_verification

Type: claim_segmentation; flags: AMBIGUOUS_SEGMENTATION

Exact report/unit text:

社区帖子引用 OpenAI 数据保护附录称,OpenAI 可继续处理源自客户数据的去标识化、匿名化或聚合信息,以改进 OpenAI 的系统和服务

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: factual

保留‘社区帖子引用’归因；不能把带限定语的引述升级成已核实合同解释。

Uncertainty: 人工确认限定语与分句继承范围。

Parent report sentence: 社区帖子引用 OpenAI 数据保护附录称,OpenAI 可继续处理源自客户数据的去标识化、匿名化或聚合信息,以改进 OpenAI 的系统和服务;该条款与 API 默认不用于训练或改进的表述之间存在边界争议。

Evidence: ev_b08a10e340e4cca0 — https://community.openai.com/t/api-is-our-data-really-ours-major-concern-in-data-processing-addendum/773047

```text
API - Is our data really "ours"? Major Concern in Data Processing Addendum - Community - OpenAI Developer Community
API - Is our data really "ours"? Major Concern in Data Processing Addendum
Community
api
,
privacy
,
legal
naferreira_7
May 22, 2024, 5:57pm
1
OpenAI clearly says that “
We do not train on your business data (data from ChatGPT Team, ChatGPT Enterprise, or our API Platform)
”, and that “
you own your inputs and outputs
”.
However, in the data processing addendum at
https://openai.com/policies/data-processing-addendum/
, which regulates data processing for the API and ChatGPT Enterprise, there is a clause that says:
“
For clarity, OpenAI may continue to process information derived from Customer Data that has been deidentified, anonymized, and/or aggregated such that the data is no longer considered Personal Data under applicable Data Protection Laws and in a manner that does not identify individuals or Customer to improve OpenAI’s systems and services.
”
```

Evidence: ev_d384aee29a4051c8 — https://community.openai.com/t/api-is-our-data-really-ours-major-concern-in-data-processing-addendum/773047

```text
For clarity, OpenAI may continue to process information derived from Customer Data that has been deidentified, anonymized, and/or aggregated such that the data is no longer considered Personal Data under applicable Data Protection Laws and in a manner that does not identify individuals or Customer to improve OpenAI’s systems and services.
In context, it looks like they’re talking about metadata that still exists after the data itself is deleted e.g. logs of request times, usage amounts, etc. I could be wrong, but the section it was a part of is relevant.
lijianathome
April 7, 2025, 4:03am
9
Sorry but I’d assume this has been a standard approach in the industry since Internet if not longer, whether end users or organizations like it or not? Or Im missing something obvious please?
```

Evidence: ev_d2c625b8589d3de9 — https://openai.xiniushu.com/policies/api-data-usage-policies

```text
数据使用政策 | OpenAI 官方帮助文档中文版
跳到主要内容
本页总览
数据使用政策
从2023年3月1日开始，我们对数据使用和保留政策做出了两项更改：
除非您明确决定与我们共享您的数据，否则OpenAI将不会使用通过我们的API提交的数据来训练或改进我们的模型。您可以选择选择加入以共享数据。
通过API发送的任何数据将被保留用于滥用和误用监控目的，最长时间为30天，之后将被删除（除非法律另有规定）。
OpenAI API处理用户提示和完成请求，以及通过“Files”端点提交的训练数据以微调模型。我们将此类数据称为API数据。
默认情况下，OpenAI不会使用通过我们的API提交的数据来训练OpenAI模型或改善OpenAI的服务提供。用户提交的用于微调的数据仅用于微调客户的模型。但是，OpenAI将允许用户选择选择加入共享他们的数据以提高模型性能。共享您的数据将确保模型的未来迭代针对您的用例得到改进。在此更改生效之前的2023年3月1日之前通过API提交的数据，如果客户之前没有选择退出共享数据，则可能已用于改进。
OpenAI为了滥用和误用监控目的保留API数据30天。少数经过授权的OpenAI员工以及受保密和安全义务约束的专业第三方承包商可以访问此数据，仅用于调查和验证涉嫌滥用行为。部署低误用可能性用例的企业客户可以请求根本不存储API数据，包括用于安全监控和预防。OpenAI仍可能有内容分类器标记涉嫌包含平台滥用的数据。例如，通过“Files”端点由用户提交的用于微调模型的数据将保留，直到用户删除该文件。
请注意，此数据政策不适用于OpenAI的非API消费者服务，如ChatGPT或DALL·E实验室。您可以在我们的
消费者服务数据使用FAQ
中了解更多信息。
常见问题解答
​
OpenAI的公共API拥有哪些技术保护和安全认证？
​
OpenAI符合SOC 2 Type 1标准，并已经通过独立第三方审核，符合2017年安全信托服务标准。
API数据存储在哪里？
​
内容存储在OpenAI系统和我们的子处理器系统中。我们还可能向第三方承包商发送部分去标识化的内容（受保密和安全义务约束）以确保安全。我们的30天数据保留政策也适用于我们的子处理器和承包商。您可以查看我们的子处理器列表以了解其位置和详细信息。
当我调用API时，数据是否在传输过程中加密？
​
```

Evidence: ev_2031367fc36e847d — https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/your-data

```text
Try ChatGPT
Copy Page
Understand how OpenAI uses your data, and how you can control it.
Your data is your data. As of March 1, 2023, data sent to the OpenAI API is not used to train or improve OpenAI models (unless you explicitly opt in to share data with us).
Types of data stored with the OpenAI API
When using the OpenAI API, data may be stored as:
Abuse monitoring logs:
Logs generated from your use of the platform, necessary for OpenAI to enforce our
Usage Policies
and agreements and mitigate harmful uses of AI.
Application state:
Data persisted from some API features in order to fulfill the task or request.
Data retention controls for abuse monitoring
```

## EIV2_FV_001-C006

Case/category: EIV2_FV_001 / factual_verification

Type: claim_segmentation; flags:

Exact report/unit text:

某些 API 功能会为完成请求或任务而持久化数据。

Evidence: ev_b08a10e340e4cca0 — https://community.openai.com/t/api-is-our-data-really-ours-major-concern-in-data-processing-addendum/773047

```text
api

naferreira_7
May 22, 2024, 5:57pm
1
OpenAI clearly says that “
We do not train on your business data (data from ChatGPT Team, ChatGPT Enterprise, or our API Platform)
”, and that “
you own your inputs and outputs
”.
However, in the data processing addendum at
https://openai.com/policies/data-processing-addendum/
, which regulates data processing for the API and ChatGPT Enterprise, there is a clause that says:
“
For clarity, OpenAI may continue to process information derived from Customer Data that has been deidentified, anonymized, and/or aggregated such that the data is no longer considered Personal Data under applicable Data Protection Laws and in a manner that does not identify individuals or Customer to improve OpenAI’s systems and services.
”
```

Evidence: ev_d2c625b8589d3de9 — https://openai.xiniushu.com/policies/api-data-usage-policies

```text
数据使用政策 | OpenAI 官方帮助文档中文版
跳到主要内容
本页总览
数据使用政策
从2023年3月1日开始，我们对数据使用和保留政策做出了两项更改：
除非您明确决定与我们共享您的数据，否则OpenAI将不会使用通过我们的API提交的数据来训练或改进我们的模型。您可以选择选择加入以共享数据。
通过API发送的任何数据将被保留用于滥用和误用监控目的，最长时间为30天，之后将被删除（除非法律另有规定）。
OpenAI API处理用户提示和完成请求，以及通过“Files”端点提交的训练数据以微调模型。我们将此类数据称为API数据。
默认情况下，OpenAI不会使用通过我们的API提交的数据来训练OpenAI模型或改善OpenAI的服务提供。用户提交的用于微调的数据仅用于微调客户的模型。但是，OpenAI将允许用户选择选择加入共享他们的数据以提高模型性能。共享您的数据将确保模型的未来迭代针对您的用例得到改进。在此更改生效之前的2023年3月1日之前通过API提交的数据，如果客户之前没有选择退出共享数据，则可能已用于改进。
OpenAI为了滥用和误用监控目的保留API数据30天。少数经过授权的OpenAI员工以及受保密和安全义务约束的专业第三方承包商可以访问此数据，仅用于调查和验证涉嫌滥用行为。部署低误用可能性用例的企业客户可以请求根本不存储API数据，包括用于安全监控和预防。OpenAI仍可能有内容分类器标记涉嫌包含平台滥用的数据。例如，通过“Files”端点由用户提交的用于微调模型的数据将保留，直到用户删除该文件。
请注意，此数据政策不适用于OpenAI的非API消费者服务，如ChatGPT或DALL·E实验室。您可以在我们的
消费者服务数据使用FAQ
中了解更多信息。
常见问题解答
​
OpenAI的公共API拥有哪些技术保护和安全认证？
​
OpenAI符合SOC 2 Type 1标准，并已经通过独立第三方审核，符合2017年安全信托服务标准。
API数据存储在哪里？
​
内容存储在OpenAI系统和我们的子处理器系统中。我们还可能向第三方承包商发送部分去标识化的内容（受保密和安全义务约束）以确保安全。我们的30天数据保留政策也适用于我们的子处理器和承包商。您可以查看我们的子处理器列表以了解其位置和详细信息。
当我调用API时，数据是否在传输过程中加密？
​
```

只保存默认政策说明，没有争议发生或裁定内容。

Parent report sentence: 社区帖子引用 OpenAI 数据保护附录称,OpenAI 可继续处理源自客户数据的去标识化、匿名化或聚合信息,以改进 OpenAI 的系统和服务;该条款与 API 默认不用于训练或改进的表述之间存在边界争议。

Evidence: ev_d2c625b8589d3de9 — https://openai.xiniushu.com/policies/api-data-usage-policies

```text
数据使用政策 | OpenAI 官方帮助文档中文版
跳到主要内容
本页总览
数据使用政策
从2023年3月1日开始，我们对数据使用和保留政策做出了两项更改：
除非您明确决定与我们共享您的数据，否则OpenAI将不会使用通过我们的API提交的数据来训练或改进我们的模型。您可以选择选择加入以共享数据。
通过API发送的任何数据将被保留用于滥用和误用监控目的，最长时间为30天，之后将被删除（除非法律另有规定）。
OpenAI API处理用户提示和完成请求，以及通过“Files”端点提交的训练数据以微调模型。我们将此类数据称为API数据。
默认情况下，OpenAI不会使用通过我们的API提交的数据来训练OpenAI模型或改善OpenAI的服务提供。用户提交的用于微调的数据仅用于微调客户的模型。但是，OpenAI将允许用户选择选择加入共享他们的数据以提高模型性能。共享您的数据将确保模型的未来迭代针对您的用例得到改进。在此更改生效之前的2023年3月1日之前通过API提交的数据，如果客户之前没有选择退出共享数据，则可能已用于改进。
OpenAI为了滥用和误用监控目的保留API数据30天。少数经过授权的OpenAI员工以及受保密和安全义务约束的专业第三方承包商可以访问此数据，仅用于调查和验证涉嫌滥用行为。部署低误用可能性用例的企业客户可以请求根本不存储API数据，包括用于安全监控和预防。OpenAI仍可能有内容分类器标记涉嫌包含平台滥用的数据。例如，通过“Files”端点由用户提交的用于微调模型的数据将保留，直到用户删除该文件。
请注意，此数据政策不适用于OpenAI的非API消费者服务，如ChatGPT或DALL·E实验室。您可以在我们的
消费者服务数据使用FAQ
中了解更多信息。
常见问题解答
​
OpenAI的公共API拥有哪些技术保护和安全认证？
​
OpenAI符合SOC 2 Type 1标准，并已经通过独立第三方审核，符合2017年安全信托服务标准。
API数据存储在哪里？
​
内容存储在OpenAI系统和我们的子处理器系统中。我们还可能向第三方承包商发送部分去标识化的内容（受保密和安全义务约束）以确保安全。我们的30天数据保留政策也适用于我们的子处理器和承包商。您可以查看我们的子处理器列表以了解其位置和详细信息。
当我调用API时，数据是否在传输过程中加密？
​
```

```text
Understand how OpenAI uses your data, and how you can control it.

Types of data stored with the OpenAI API

Abuse monitoring logs:
Logs generated from your use of the platform, necessary for OpenAI to enforce our
Usage Policies
```

Evidence: ev_b08a10e340e4cca0 — https://community.openai.com/t/api-is-our-data-really-ours-major-concern-in-data-processing-addendum/773047

```text

api

naferreira_7
May 22, 2024, 5:57pm
1
OpenAI clearly says that “
We do not train on your business data (data from ChatGPT Team, ChatGPT Enterprise, or our API Platform)
”, and that “
you own your inputs and outputs
”.
However, in the data processing addendum at
https://openai.com/policies/data-processing-addendum/
, which regulates data processing for the API and ChatGPT Enterprise, there is a clause that says:
“
For clarity, OpenAI may continue to process information derived from Customer Data that has been deidentified, anonymized, and/or aggregated such that the data is no longer considered Personal Data under applicable Data Protection Laws and in a manner that does not identify individuals or Customer to improve OpenAI’s systems and services.
”
```

Evidence: ev_d384aee29a4051c8 — https://community.openai.com/t/api-is-our-data-really-ours-major-concern-in-data-processing-addendum/773047

```text
For clarity, OpenAI may continue to process information derived from Customer Data that has been deidentified, anonymized, and/or aggregated such that the data is no longer considered Personal Data under applicable Data Protection Laws and in a manner that does not identify individuals or Customer to improve OpenAI’s systems and services.
In context, it looks like they’re talking about metadata that still exists after the data itself is deleted e.g. logs of request times, usage amounts, etc. I could be wrong, but the section it was a part of is relevant.
```

```text
OpenAI clearly says that “

you own your inputs and outputs

However, in the data processing addendum at

”
```

Evidence: ev_d2c625b8589d3de9 — https://openai.xiniushu.com/policies/api-data-usage-policies

```text
数据使用政策 | OpenAI 官方帮助文档中文版
跳到主要内容
本页总览
数据使用政策
从2023年3月1日开始，我们对数据使用和保留政策做出了两项更改：
除非您明确决定与我们共享您的数据，否则OpenAI将不会使用通过我们的API提交的数据来训练或改进我们的模型。您可以选择选择加入以共享数据。
通过API发送的任何数据将被保留用于滥用和误用监控目的，最长时间为30天，之后将被删除（除非法律另有规定）。
OpenAI API处理用户提示和完成请求，以及通过“Files”端点提交的训练数据以微调模型。我们将此类数据称为API数据。
默认情况下，OpenAI不会使用通过我们的API提交的数据来训练OpenAI模型或改善OpenAI的服务提供。用户提交的用于微调的数据仅用于微调客户的模型。但是，OpenAI将允许用户选择选择加入共享他们的数据以提高模型性能。共享您的数据将确保模型的未来迭代针对您的用例得到改进。在此更改生效之前的2023年3月1日之前通过API提交的数据，如果客户之前没有选择退出共享数据，则可能已用于改进。
OpenAI为了滥用和误用监控目的保留API数据30天。少数经过授权的OpenAI员工以及受保密和安全义务约束的专业第三方承包商可以访问此数据，仅用于调查和验证涉嫌滥用行为。部署低误用可能性用例的企业客户可以请求根本不存储API数据，包括用于安全监控和预防。OpenAI仍可能有内容分类器标记涉嫌包含平台滥用的数据。例如，通过“Files”端点由用户提交的用于微调模型的数据将保留，直到用户删除该文件。
请注意，此数据政策不适用于OpenAI的非API消费者服务，如ChatGPT或DALL·E实验室。您可以在我们的
消费者服务数据使用FAQ
中了解更多信息。
常见问题解答
​
OpenAI的公共API拥有哪些技术保护和安全认证？
​
OpenAI符合SOC 2 Type 1标准，并已经通过独立第三方审核，符合2017年安全信托服务标准。
API数据存储在哪里？
​
内容存储在OpenAI系统和我们的子处理器系统中。我们还可能向第三方承包商发送部分去标识化的内容（受保密和安全义务约束）以确保安全。我们的30天数据保留政策也适用于我们的子处理器和承包商。您可以查看我们的子处理器列表以了解其位置和详细信息。
当我调用API时，数据是否在传输过程中加密？
​
```

```text
Types of data stored with the OpenAI API
When using the OpenAI API, data may be stored as:
Abuse monitoring logs:

Usage Policies
and agreements and mitigate harmful uses of AI.
```

```text
May 22, 2024, 5:57pm

”
```

Evidence: ev_d2c625b8589d3de9 — https://openai.xiniushu.com/policies/api-data-usage-policies

```text
数据使用政策 | OpenAI 官方帮助文档中文版
跳到主要内容
本页总览
数据使用政策
从2023年3月1日开始，我们对数据使用和保留政策做出了两项更改：
除非您明确决定与我们共享您的数据，否则OpenAI将不会使用通过我们的API提交的数据来训练或改进我们的模型。您可以选择选择加入以共享数据。
通过API发送的任何数据将被保留用于滥用和误用监控目的，最长时间为30天，之后将被删除（除非法律另有规定）。
OpenAI API处理用户提示和完成请求，以及通过“Files”端点提交的训练数据以微调模型。我们将此类数据称为API数据。
默认情况下，OpenAI不会使用通过我们的API提交的数据来训练OpenAI模型或改善OpenAI的服务提供。用户提交的用于微调的数据仅用于微调客户的模型。但是，OpenAI将允许用户选择选择加入共享他们的数据以提高模型性能。共享您的数据将确保模型的未来迭代针对您的用例得到改进。在此更改生效之前的2023年3月1日之前通过API提交的数据，如果客户之前没有选择退出共享数据，则可能已用于改进。
OpenAI为了滥用和误用监控目的保留API数据30天。少数经过授权的OpenAI员工以及受保密和安全义务约束的专业第三方承包商可以访问此数据，仅用于调查和验证涉嫌滥用行为。部署低误用可能性用例的企业客户可以请求根本不存储API数据，包括用于安全监控和预防。OpenAI仍可能有内容分类器标记涉嫌包含平台滥用的数据。例如，通过“Files”端点由用户提交的用于微调模型的数据将保留，直到用户删除该文件。
请注意，此数据政策不适用于OpenAI的非API消费者服务，如ChatGPT或DALL·E实验室。您可以在我们的
消费者服务数据使用FAQ
中了解更多信息。
常见问题解答
​
OpenAI的公共API拥有哪些技术保护和安全认证？
​
OpenAI符合SOC 2 Type 1标准，并已经通过独立第三方审核，符合2017年安全信托服务标准。
API数据存储在哪里？
​
内容存储在OpenAI系统和我们的子处理器系统中。我们还可能向第三方承包商发送部分去标识化的内容（受保密和安全义务约束）以确保安全。我们的30天数据保留政策也适用于我们的子处理器和承包商。您可以查看我们的子处理器列表以了解其位置和详细信息。
当我调用API时，数据是否在传输过程中加密？
​
```

```text
数据使用政策
从2023年3月1日开始，我们对数据使用和保留政策做出了两项更改：
除非您明确决定与我们共享您的数据，否则OpenAI将不会使用通过我们的API提交的数据来训练或改进我们的模型。您可以选择选择加入以共享数据。
通过API发送的任何数据将被保留用于滥用和误用监控目的，最长时间为30天，之后将被删除（除非法律另有规定）。
OpenAI API处理用户提示和完成请求，以及通过“Files”端点提交的训练数据以微调模型。我们将此类数据称为API数据。
默认情况下，OpenAI不会使用通过我们的API提交的数据来训练OpenAI模型或改善OpenAI的服务提供。用户提交的用于微调的数据仅用于微调客户的模型。但是，OpenAI将允许用户选择选择加入共享他们的数据以提高模型性能。共享您的数据将确保模型的未来迭代针对您的用例得到改进。在此更改生效之前的2023年3月1日之前通过API提交的数据，如果客户之前没有选择退出共享数据，则可能已用于改进。
OpenAI为了滥用和误用监控目的保留API数据30天。少数经过授权的OpenAI员工以及受保密和安全义务约束的专业第三方承包商可以访问此数据，仅用于调查和验证涉嫌滥用行为。部署低误用可能性用例的企业客户可以请求根本不存储API数据，包括用于安全监控和预防。OpenAI仍可能有内容分类器标记涉嫌包含平台滥用的数据。例如，通过“Files”端点由用户提交的用于微调模型的数据将保留，直到用户删除该文件。
请注意，此数据政策不适用于OpenAI的非API消费者服务，如ChatGPT或DALL·E实验室。您可以在我们的
消费者服务数据使用FAQ
中了解更多信息。
常见问题解答
​
OpenAI的公共API拥有哪些技术保护和安全认证？
​
OpenAI符合SOC 2 Type 1标准，并已经通过独立第三方审核，符合2017年安全信托服务标准。
API数据存储在哪里？
​
内容存储在OpenAI系统和我们的子处理器系统中。我们还可能向第三方承包商发送部分去标识化的内容（受保密和安全义务约束）以确保安全。我们的30天数据保留政策也适用于我们的子处理器和承包商。您可以查看我们的子处理器列表以了解其位置和详细信息。
当我调用API时，数据是否在传输过程中加密？
​
```

## EIV2_TC_001-C001

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: claim_segmentation; flags:

Exact report/unit text:

Hybrid search in pgvector contexts combines vector\-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: factual

混合检索组成是一个机制断言。

Uncertainty: 预标注需人工确认，尚非 gold。

Parent report sentence: Hybrid search in pgvector contexts combines vector\-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering because vector search handles semantic meaning but can fail on specific keywords or structured constraints\.

Evidence: ev_62fa0fb7bbfd9bb8 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
by expanding what can be achieved using PostgreSQL alone.
What Is Hybrid Search?
Hybrid search combines vector-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering to deliver highly relevant results. While vector search leverages embeddings to find items that are semantically similar, it often lacks precision when users need to constrain their results using structured attributes or keywords.
Hybrid search addresses this gap by enabling both types of queries in a single operation, providing a more refined and context-aware retrieval experience. In this model, a typical query might first filter a dataset based on categorical information or keywords, then apply vector similarity to rank or further narrow the results.
```

Evidence: ev_7a6809120ded0e6a — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
This approach is valuable when dealing with real-world data, where both the semantic meaning and structured attributes are important. Tools like pgvector make hybrid search feasible within PostgreSQL, allowing developers to combine modern AI-powered methods with classic SQL querying.
This is part of a series of articles about
vector database
Benefits of Using Pgvector for Hybrid Search
Using
pgvector
for hybrid search in PostgreSQL environments offers several practical advantages:
Single-platform integration
: pgvector allows teams to manage both structured data and vector embeddings in the same PostgreSQL instance. This eliminates the need for separate vector databases or synchronization layers.
Simplified query logic
: Hybrid search queries can be expressed using standard SQL syntax, combining traditional filters (WHERE, JOIN, etc.) with vector similarity functions (<->). This makes complex queries more maintainable and easier to debug.
Reduced latency
```

Evidence: ev_4426f461ab217c20 — https://medium.com/@aysebilgegunduz/all-you-need-to-know-about-pgvector-part-4-hybrid-search-8a655d3b0087

```text
Why pgvector (and not a dedicated vector DB)
Every team building RAG hits the same fork in the road: do I spin up Pinecone / Weaviate / Qdrant, or do I just add a vector column to my existing Postgres? In 2023 the answer leaned toward dedicated vector DBs. In 2026 pgvector has caught up on every metric that matters for <10M vector workloads — and crushes everyone on operational simplicity.
```

Evidence: ev_4b2cc9add6171347 — https://www.paradedb.com/learn/postgresql/pgvector-limitations

```text
Summary
pgvector is the right choice when your dataset fits comfortably on one PostgreSQL instance, your filter selectivity is moderate, and you value transactional consistency with the rest of your relational data. The edges to watch are index build cost, filtered query performance, missing hybrid and reranking primitives, and the absence of horizontal scaling. Knowing where those edges sit makes it easier to decide whether pgvector is sufficient on its own, whether to pair it with
full-text search
for
hybrid retrieval
, or whether part of the workload belongs in a dedicated engine.
Previous
Next
```

Type: claim_segmentation; flags: AMBIGUOUS_SEGMENTATION

Exact report/unit text:

filtered query performance

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: factual

从同一句的 limits 列表分离一个限制，主语及限定范围继承原句。

Uncertainty: 人工确认限定语与分句继承范围。

Parent report sentence: pgvector is the right choice when the dataset fits comfortably on one PostgreSQL instance, filter selectivity is moderate, and transactional consistency with relational data is valued; the edges to watch include index build cost, filtered query performance, missing hybrid and reranking primitives, and absence of horizontal scaling\.

Evidence: ev_4b2cc9add6171347 — https://www.paradedb.com/learn/postgresql/pgvector-limitations

```text
Summary
pgvector is the right choice when your dataset fits comfortably on one PostgreSQL instance, your filter selectivity is moderate, and you value transactional consistency with the rest of your relational data. The edges to watch are index build cost, filtered query performance, missing hybrid and reranking primitives, and the absence of horizontal scaling. Knowing where those edges sit makes it easier to decide whether pgvector is sufficient on its own, whether to pair it with
full-text search
for
hybrid retrieval
, or whether part of the workload belongs in a dedicated engine.

Next
```

## EIV2_TC_001-C012

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: claim_segmentation; flags:

Exact report/unit text:

Managing pgvector in production requires PostgreSQL expertise

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: factual

运维专业知识要求独立于下列具体限制。

Uncertainty: 预标注需人工确认，尚非 gold。

Parent report sentence: Managing pgvector in production requires PostgreSQL expertise: vacuum operations become more complex with large vector tables, index maintenance requires downtime or careful online rebuilding, monitoring vector\-specific performance requires custom instrumentation, and backup and restore times increase significantly with vector data\.

Evidence: ev_648babff2ebe3058 — https://www.velodb.io/glossary/what-is-pgvector

```text
Embedding Model Dependency
Search quality depends entirely on your upstream embedding model. pgvector stores and retrieves vectors—it doesn't understand or improve the semantic quality of your embeddings. Poor embeddings produce poor results regardless of database performance.
Operational Complexity at Scale
Managing pgvector in production requires PostgreSQL expertise:
Vacuum operations become more complex with large vector tables
Index maintenance requires downtime or careful online rebuilding
Monitoring vector-specific performance requires custom instrumentation
Backup and restore times increase significantly with vector data
When Should You Choose pgvector?
pgvector fits specific scenarios well. Consider it when:
You already run PostgreSQL
: The extension adds vector capabilities without new infrastructure, operational complexity, or vendor relationships.
Vector search is a feature, not the core product
```

Evidence: ev_24320d0559472fcb — https://www.velodb.io/glossary/what-is-pgvector

```text
Monitoring
Track these metrics for vector workloads:
Index size and build time
Query latency percentiles (p50, p95, p99)
Recall rate (requires ground truth comparison)
Connection pool utilization during vector queries
Backup Considerations
Vector columns increase backup sizes significantly. Consider:
Incremental backup strategies
Separate tablespaces for vector data
Testing restore times with realistic data volumes
Frequently Asked Questions About pgvector
Do I need a separate database to use pgvector?
No. pgvector is an extension that installs into your existing PostgreSQL database. Enable it with
CREATE EXTENSION vector;
and you can immediately create vector columns in any table. Your vectors and relational data share the same database instance, transactions, and backup procedures.
Is pgvector a vector database?
```

## EIV2_TC_001-C017

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Filtered vector query performance is a practical edge for pgvector

性能限制的定性陈述仍需适当佐证。

Uncertainty: 预标注需人工确认，尚非 gold。

Parent report sentence: Filtered vector query performance is a practical edge for pgvector; one source recommends pgvector when filter selectivity is moderate and warns that filtered query performance, index build cost, and missing hybrid primitives are limits\.

Evidence: ev_4b2cc9add6171347 — https://www.paradedb.com/learn/postgresql/pgvector-limitations

```text
Summary
pgvector is the right choice when your dataset fits comfortably on one PostgreSQL instance, your filter selectivity is moderate, and you value transactional consistency with the rest of your relational data. The edges to watch are index build cost, filtered query performance, missing hybrid and reranking primitives, and the absence of horizontal scaling. Knowing where those edges sit makes it easier to decide whether pgvector is sufficient on its own, whether to pair it with
full-text search
for
hybrid retrieval
, or whether part of the workload belongs in a dedicated engine.
Previous
Next
```

## EIV2_TC_001-C022

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: claim_segmentation; flags: AMBIGUOUS_SEGMENTATION

Exact report/unit text:

enterprise search

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: factual

拆分有用场景列表；每项继承原句 pgvector hybrid search is described as useful for 限定。RAG 只有截断小节标题，不能确认完整论据。

Uncertainty: 人工确认限定语与分句继承范围。

Parent report sentence: pgvector hybrid search is described as useful for enterprise search, document retrieval in knowledge bases, and RAG because it can filter by structured attributes such as department, author, date, or document type while using vector embeddings for semantic ranking\.

Evidence: ev_a68505e19318d340 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
Document Retrieval in Knowledge Bases
In large knowledge bases or documentation portals, finding the right piece of information can be challenging due to varied terminology, document formats, or organizational silos. Hybrid search with pgvector enables users to filter by document type, author, or date, while also harnessing vector embeddings to find content semantically related to a user’s query. This approach bridges gaps between keywords and actual user intent.
For enterprises, this capability reduces time spent searching and improves knowledge discovery. Team members can locate conceptually related guides, policies, or troubleshooting steps without needing to know exact phrases.
RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation)
```

Evidence: ev_c87ab4cb76d42dd2 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
Enterprise Search Applications
Organizations often amass vast volumes of structured and unstructured data (emails, reports, contracts, etc.). Hybrid search with pgvector equips internal search engines to surface relevant results across heterogeneous document types by marrying SQL-based filtering (department, author, date) with vector-driven semantic ranking. This improves the search experience for employees, ensuring they retrieve not only exact matches but also contextually relevant documents.
By keeping metadata and embeddings together in PostgreSQL, enterprises maintain robust governance and simplified access control. IT teams can leverage familiar backup, monitoring, and disaster recovery workflows while building advanced search capabilities. The result is more efficient information retrieval, improved productivity, and secure knowledge management.
Related content: Read our guide to Pgvector performance (coming soon)
Tips from the expert
Perry Clark
```

Evidence: ev_62fa0fb7bbfd9bb8 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
by expanding what can be achieved using PostgreSQL alone.
What Is Hybrid Search?
Hybrid search combines vector-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering to deliver highly relevant results. While vector search leverages embeddings to find items that are semantically similar, it often lacks precision when users need to constrain their results using structured attributes or keywords.
Hybrid search addresses this gap by enabling both types of queries in a single operation, providing a more refined and context-aware retrieval experience. In this model, a typical query might first filter a dataset based on categorical information or keywords, then apply vector similarity to rank or further narrow the results.
```

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: factual

拆分有用场景列表；每项继承原句 pgvector hybrid search is described as useful for 限定。RAG 只有截断小节标题，不能确认完整论据。

Uncertainty: 人工确认限定语与分句继承范围。

Parent report sentence: pgvector hybrid search is described as useful for enterprise search, document retrieval in knowledge bases, and RAG because it can filter by structured attributes such as department, author, date, or document type while using vector embeddings for semantic ranking\.

Evidence: ev_a68505e19318d340 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
Document Retrieval in Knowledge Bases
In large knowledge bases or documentation portals, finding the right piece of information can be challenging due to varied terminology, document formats, or organizational silos. Hybrid search with pgvector enables users to filter by document type, author, or date, while also harnessing vector embeddings to find content semantically related to a user’s query. This approach bridges gaps between keywords and actual user intent.
For enterprises, this capability reduces time spent searching and improves knowledge discovery. Team members can locate conceptually related guides, policies, or troubleshooting steps without needing to know exact phrases.
RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation)
```

Evidence: ev_c87ab4cb76d42dd2 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
Enterprise Search Applications
Organizations often amass vast volumes of structured and unstructured data (emails, reports, contracts, etc.). Hybrid search with pgvector equips internal search engines to surface relevant results across heterogeneous document types by marrying SQL-based filtering (department, author, date) with vector-driven semantic ranking. This improves the search experience for employees, ensuring they retrieve not only exact matches but also contextually relevant documents.
By keeping metadata and embeddings together in PostgreSQL, enterprises maintain robust governance and simplified access control. IT teams can leverage familiar backup, monitoring, and disaster recovery workflows while building advanced search capabilities. The result is more efficient information retrieval, improved productivity, and secure knowledge management.
Related content: Read our guide to Pgvector performance (coming soon)
Tips from the expert
Perry Clark
```

Evidence: ev_62fa0fb7bbfd9bb8 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
by expanding what can be achieved using PostgreSQL alone.
What Is Hybrid Search?
Hybrid search combines vector-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering to deliver highly relevant results. While vector search leverages embeddings to find items that are semantically similar, it often lacks precision when users need to constrain their results using structured attributes or keywords.
Hybrid search addresses this gap by enabling both types of queries in a single operation, providing a more refined and context-aware retrieval experience. In this model, a typical query might first filter a dataset based on categorical information or keywords, then apply vector similarity to rank or further narrow the results.
```

## EIV2_TC_001-C029

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: claim_segmentation; flags:

Exact report/unit text:

The PostgreSQL query planner can use pgvector indexes for nearest\-neighbor queries

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: factual

查询规划器使用索引的断言单独评估。

Uncertainty: 预标注需人工确认，尚非 gold。

Parent report sentence: The PostgreSQL query planner can use pgvector indexes for nearest\-neighbor queries, and vector search can be combined with standard SQL filters, joins, and CTEs in the same query\.

Evidence: ev_9c01b4455145f8f5 — https://www.tigerdata.com/blog/the-postgres-developers-guide-to-vector-index-tradeoffs

```text
An HNSW index looks like this:
CREATE INDEX ON documents
USING hnsw (embedding vector_cosine_ops);
For IVFFlat, you define the number of lists and tune the number of probes:
CREATE INDEX ON documents
USING ivfflat (embedding vector_cosine_ops)
WITH (lists = 1000);
SET ivfflat.probes = 10;
The query planner can use these indexes for nearest-neighbor queries, and you can combine vector search with standard SQL filters, joins and CTEs in the same query. For many teams already running Postgres, this can remove the need to operate a separate vector database.
pgvector
can start to show limits at larger scale, especially with high-dimensional embeddings at tens of millions of rows and indexes that no longer fit comfortably in memory. That is the problem
pgvectorscale
was built to address.
pgvectorscale
The DiskANN section above describes a specific problem: vector workloads that have grown too large to keep the working index in memory. For Postgres,
pgvectorscale
```

Evidence: ev_e38e6e3a23869cb1 — https://www.velodb.io/glossary/what-is-pgvector

```text
Query Patterns and Performance Characteristics
pgvector queries integrate naturally with SQL, enabling powerful hybrid searches:
SELECT
d.content, d.embedding
<=>
query_embedding
AS
similarity
FROM
documents d
JOIN
categories c
ON
d.category_id
=
c.id
WHERE
c.name
=
'technology'
AND
d.created_at
>
'2024-01-01'
ORDER
BY
similarity
LIMIT
20
;
This combines vector similarity with relational filters and joins—something that requires complex orchestration with standalone vector databases.
Performance scales with dataset size, vector dimensions, and index configuration. For datasets under 1 million vectors with proper indexing, pgvector typically delivers sub-second query latency.
Why Developers Use pgvector
pgvector has gained significant adoption for several practical reasons:
Unified Infrastructure
No need to deploy, manage, or pay for a separate vector database. Your vectors live alongside application data in the same PostgreSQL instance you already operate.
Familiar SQL Interface
```

reviewer_identity: null; human_decision: null; review_timestamp: null; adjudication_note: null

```text
For IVFFlat, you define the number of lists and tune the number of probes:
CREATE INDEX ON documents
USING ivfflat (embedding vector_cosine_ops)
WITH (lists = 1000);
SET ivfflat.probes = 10;
The query planner can use these indexes for nearest-neighbor queries, and you can combine vector search with standard SQL filters, joins and CTEs in the same query. For many teams already running Postgres, this can remove the need to operate a separate vector database.
pgvector
can start to show limits at larger scale, especially with high-dimensional embeddings at tens of millions of rows and indexes that no longer fit comfortably in memory. That is the problem
pgvectorscale
was built to address.
pgvectorscale
The DiskANN section above describes a specific problem: vector workloads that have grown too large to keep the working index in memory. For Postgres,
pgvectorscale
```

Evidence: ev_e38e6e3a23869cb1 — https://www.velodb.io/glossary/what-is-pgvector

```text
Query Patterns and Performance Characteristics
pgvector queries integrate naturally with SQL, enabling powerful hybrid searches:
SELECT
d.content, d.embedding
<=>
query_embedding
AS
similarity
FROM
documents d
JOIN
categories c
ON
d.category_id
=
c.id
WHERE
c.name
=
'technology'
AND
d.created_at
>
'2024-01-01'
ORDER
BY
similarity
LIMIT
20
;
This combines vector similarity with relational filters and joins—something that requires complex orchestration with standalone vector databases.
Performance scales with dataset size, vector dimensions, and index configuration. For datasets under 1 million vectors with proper indexing, pgvector typically delivers sub-second query latency.
Why Developers Use pgvector
pgvector has gained significant adoption for several practical reasons:
Unified Infrastructure
No need to deploy, manage, or pay for a separate vector database. Your vectors live alongside application data in the same PostgreSQL instance you already operate.
Familiar SQL Interface
```

## EIV2_TC_001-C033

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: claim_segmentation; flags:

Exact report/unit text:

Vector columns increase backup sizes significantly

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: factual

备份体积显著增加属于定性性能/资源断言，未提供测量边界。

Uncertainty: 预标注需人工确认，尚非 gold。

Parent report sentence: Vector columns increase backup sizes significantly, so teams may need incremental backup strategies, separate tablespaces for vector data, and restore\-time testing with realistic data volumes\.

Evidence: ev_24320d0559472fcb — https://www.velodb.io/glossary/what-is-pgvector

```text
Monitoring
Track these metrics for vector workloads:
Index size and build time
Query latency percentiles (p50, p95, p99)
Recall rate (requires ground truth comparison)
Connection pool utilization during vector queries
Backup Considerations
Vector columns increase backup sizes significantly. Consider:
Incremental backup strategies
Separate tablespaces for vector data
Testing restore times with realistic data volumes
Frequently Asked Questions About pgvector
Do I need a separate database to use pgvector?
No. pgvector is an extension that installs into your existing PostgreSQL database. Enable it with
CREATE EXTENSION vector;
and you can immediately create vector columns in any table. Your vectors and relational data share the same database instance, transactions, and backup procedures.
Is pgvector a vector database?
```

## EIV2_TC_001-C035

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: claim_segmentation; flags: AMBIGUOUS_SEGMENTATION

Exact report/unit text:

pgvector search quality depends entirely on the upstream embedding model

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: factual

entirely 是绝对化因果范围，源文虽如此说仍须人工审核，不能当独立验证。

Uncertainty: 人工确认限定语与分句继承范围。

Parent report sentence: pgvector search quality depends entirely on the upstream embedding model; the extension stores and retrieves vectors but does not understand or improve semantic quality, so poor embeddings produce poor results regardless of database performance\.

Evidence: ev_648babff2ebe3058 — https://www.velodb.io/glossary/what-is-pgvector

```text
Embedding Model Dependency
Search quality depends entirely on your upstream embedding model. pgvector stores and retrieves vectors—it doesn't understand or improve the semantic quality of your embeddings. Poor embeddings produce poor results regardless of database performance.
Operational Complexity at Scale
Managing pgvector in production requires PostgreSQL expertise:
Vacuum operations become more complex with large vector tables
Index maintenance requires downtime or careful online rebuilding
Monitoring vector-specific performance requires custom instrumentation
Backup and restore times increase significantly with vector data
When Should You Choose pgvector?
pgvector fits specific scenarios well. Consider it when:
You already run PostgreSQL
: The extension adds vector capabilities without new infrastructure, operational complexity, or vendor relationships.
Vector search is a feature, not the core product
```

reviewer_identity: null; human_decision: null; review_timestamp: null; adjudication_note: null

## EIV2_TC_001-citation_support-001

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: citation_support; flags:

Exact report/unit text:

Hybrid search in pgvector contexts combines vector\-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: support

保存片段直接包含此断言的对应机制/用途；不把文中其他未引用断言补入支持。

Uncertainty: 文本支持建议仍待来源日期和人工确认。

Parent report sentence: Hybrid search in pgvector contexts combines vector\-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering because vector search handles semantic meaning but can fail on specific keywords or structured constraints\.

Evidence: ev_62fa0fb7bbfd9bb8 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
by expanding what can be achieved using PostgreSQL alone.
What Is Hybrid Search?
Hybrid search combines vector-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering to deliver highly relevant results. While vector search leverages embeddings to find items that are semantically similar, it often lacks precision when users need to constrain their results using structured attributes or keywords.
Hybrid search addresses this gap by enabling both types of queries in a single operation, providing a more refined and context-aware retrieval experience. In this model, a typical query might first filter a dataset based on categorical information or keywords, then apply vector similarity to rank or further narrow the results.
```

Hybrid search in pgvector contexts combines vector\-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering

Evidence: ev_7a6809120ded0e6a — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
This approach is valuable when dealing with real-world data, where both the semantic meaning and structured attributes are important. Tools like pgvector make hybrid search feasible within PostgreSQL, allowing developers to combine modern AI-powered methods with classic SQL querying.
This is part of a series of articles about
vector database
Benefits of Using Pgvector for Hybrid Search
Using
pgvector
for hybrid search in PostgreSQL environments offers several practical advantages:
Single-platform integration
: pgvector allows teams to manage both structured data and vector embeddings in the same PostgreSQL instance. This eliminates the need for separate vector databases or synchronization layers.
Simplified query logic
: Hybrid search queries can be expressed using standard SQL syntax, combining traditional filters (WHERE, JOIN, etc.) with vector similarity functions (<->). This makes complex queries more maintainable and easier to debug.
Reduced latency
```

```text
This approach is valuable when dealing with real-world data, where both the semantic meaning and structured attributes are important. Tools like pgvector make hybrid search feasible within PostgreSQL, allowing developers to combine modern AI-powered methods with classic SQL querying.
This is part of a series of articles about
vector database
Benefits of Using Pgvector for Hybrid Search
Using
pgvector
for hybrid search in PostgreSQL environments offers several practical advantages:
Single-platform integration
: pgvector allows teams to manage both structured data and vector embeddings in the same PostgreSQL instance. This eliminates the need for separate vector databases or synchronization layers.
Simplified query logic
: Hybrid search queries can be expressed using standard SQL syntax, combining traditional filters (WHERE, JOIN, etc.) with vector similarity functions (<->). This makes complex queries more maintainable and easier to debug.
Reduced latency
```

## EIV2_TC_001-citation_support-023

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: citation_support; flags:

Exact report/unit text:

Managing pgvector in production requires PostgreSQL expertise

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: support

保存片段直接包含此断言的对应机制/用途；不把文中其他未引用断言补入支持。

Uncertainty: 文本支持建议仍待来源日期和人工确认。

Parent report sentence: Managing pgvector in production requires PostgreSQL expertise: vacuum operations become more complex with large vector tables, index maintenance requires downtime or careful online rebuilding, monitoring vector\-specific performance requires custom instrumentation, and backup and restore times increase significantly with vector data\.

Evidence: ev_648babff2ebe3058 — https://www.velodb.io/glossary/what-is-pgvector

```text
Embedding Model Dependency
Search quality depends entirely on your upstream embedding model. pgvector stores and retrieves vectors—it doesn't understand or improve the semantic quality of your embeddings. Poor embeddings produce poor results regardless of database performance.
Operational Complexity at Scale
Managing pgvector in production requires PostgreSQL expertise:
Vacuum operations become more complex with large vector tables
Index maintenance requires downtime or careful online rebuilding
Monitoring vector-specific performance requires custom instrumentation
Backup and restore times increase significantly with vector data
When Should You Choose pgvector?
pgvector fits specific scenarios well. Consider it when:
You already run PostgreSQL
: The extension adds vector capabilities without new infrastructure, operational complexity, or vendor relationships.
Vector search is a feature, not the core product
```

reviewer_identity: null; human_decision: null; review_timestamp: null; adjudication_note: null

Managing pgvector in production requires PostgreSQL expertise

Parent report sentence: Managing pgvector in production requires PostgreSQL expertise: vacuum operations become more complex with large vector tables, index maintenance requires downtime or careful online rebuilding, monitoring vector\-specific performance requires custom instrumentation, and backup and restore times increase significantly with vector data\.

Evidence: ev_24320d0559472fcb — https://www.velodb.io/glossary/what-is-pgvector

```text
Monitoring
Track these metrics for vector workloads:
Index size and build time
Query latency percentiles (p50, p95, p99)
Recall rate (requires ground truth comparison)
Connection pool utilization during vector queries
Backup Considerations
Vector columns increase backup sizes significantly. Consider:
Incremental backup strategies
Separate tablespaces for vector data
Testing restore times with realistic data volumes
Frequently Asked Questions About pgvector
Do I need a separate database to use pgvector?
No. pgvector is an extension that installs into your existing PostgreSQL database. Enable it with
CREATE EXTENSION vector;
and you can immediately create vector columns in any table. Your vectors and relational data share the same database instance, transactions, and backup procedures.
Is pgvector a vector database?
```

```text
Monitoring
Track these metrics for vector workloads:
Index size and build time
Query latency percentiles (p50, p95, p99)
Recall rate (requires ground truth comparison)

Backup Considerations
Vector columns increase backup sizes significantly. Consider:

Separate tablespaces for vector data
Testing restore times with realistic data volumes
Frequently Asked Questions About pgvector
Do I need a separate database to use pgvector?
No. pgvector is an extension that installs into your existing PostgreSQL database. Enable it with
CREATE EXTENSION vector;
and you can immediately create vector columns in any table. Your vectors and relational data share the same database instance, transactions, and backup procedures.
Is pgvector a vector database?
```

## EIV2_TC_001-citation_support-033

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: citation_support; flags:

Exact report/unit text:

Filtered vector query performance is a practical edge for pgvector

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: support

保存片段直接包含此断言的对应机制/用途；不把文中其他未引用断言补入支持。

Uncertainty: 文本支持建议仍待来源日期和人工确认。

Parent report sentence: Filtered vector query performance is a practical edge for pgvector; one source recommends pgvector when filter selectivity is moderate and warns that filtered query performance, index build cost, and missing hybrid primitives are limits\.

Evidence: ev_4b2cc9add6171347 — https://www.paradedb.com/learn/postgresql/pgvector-limitations

```text
Summary
pgvector is the right choice when your dataset fits comfortably on one PostgreSQL instance, your filter selectivity is moderate, and you value transactional consistency with the rest of your relational data. The edges to watch are index build cost, filtered query performance, missing hybrid and reranking primitives, and the absence of horizontal scaling. Knowing where those edges sit makes it easier to decide whether pgvector is sufficient on its own, whether to pair it with
full-text search
for
hybrid retrieval
, or whether part of the workload belongs in a dedicated engine.
Previous
Next
```

## EIV2_TC_001-citation_support-038

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: citation_support; flags: CITATION_SUPPORT_UNCERTAIN

Exact report/unit text:

enterprise search

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: unclear

此引用 chunk 未建立该原子断言的完整技术范围；不给部分支持分。

Uncertainty: 保存片段未建立完整范围；不给部分支持分。

Parent report sentence: pgvector hybrid search is described as useful for enterprise search, document retrieval in knowledge bases, and RAG because it can filter by structured attributes such as department, author, date, or document type while using vector embeddings for semantic ranking\.

Evidence: ev_a68505e19318d340 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
Document Retrieval in Knowledge Bases
In large knowledge bases or documentation portals, finding the right piece of information can be challenging due to varied terminology, document formats, or organizational silos. Hybrid search with pgvector enables users to filter by document type, author, or date, while also harnessing vector embeddings to find content semantically related to a user’s query. This approach bridges gaps between keywords and actual user intent.
For enterprises, this capability reduces time spent searching and improves knowledge discovery. Team members can locate conceptually related guides, policies, or troubleshooting steps without needing to know exact phrases.
RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation)
```

reviewer_identity: null; human_decision: null; review_timestamp: null; adjudication_note: null

## EIV2_TC_001-citation_support-039

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: citation_support; flags:

Exact report/unit text:

enterprise search

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: support

保存片段直接包含此断言的对应机制/用途；不把文中其他未引用断言补入支持。

Uncertainty: 文本支持建议仍待来源日期和人工确认。

Parent report sentence: pgvector hybrid search is described as useful for enterprise search, document retrieval in knowledge bases, and RAG because it can filter by structured attributes such as department, author, date, or document type while using vector embeddings for semantic ranking\.

Evidence: ev_c87ab4cb76d42dd2 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
Enterprise Search Applications
Organizations often amass vast volumes of structured and unstructured data (emails, reports, contracts, etc.). Hybrid search with pgvector equips internal search engines to surface relevant results across heterogeneous document types by marrying SQL-based filtering (department, author, date) with vector-driven semantic ranking. This improves the search experience for employees, ensuring they retrieve not only exact matches but also contextually relevant documents.
By keeping metadata and embeddings together in PostgreSQL, enterprises maintain robust governance and simplified access control. IT teams can leverage familiar backup, monitoring, and disaster recovery workflows while building advanced search capabilities. The result is more efficient information retrieval, improved productivity, and secure knowledge management.
Related content: Read our guide to Pgvector performance (coming soon)
Tips from the expert
Perry Clark
```

Evidence: ev_62fa0fb7bbfd9bb8 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
by expanding what can be achieved using PostgreSQL alone.
What Is Hybrid Search?
Hybrid search combines vector-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering to deliver highly relevant results. While vector search leverages embeddings to find items that are semantically similar, it often lacks precision when users need to constrain their results using structured attributes or keywords.
Hybrid search addresses this gap by enabling both types of queries in a single operation, providing a more refined and context-aware retrieval experience. In this model, a typical query might first filter a dataset based on categorical information or keywords, then apply vector similarity to rank or further narrow the results.
```

Type: citation_support; flags: CITATION_SUPPORT_UNCERTAIN

Exact report/unit text:

document retrieval in knowledge bases

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: unclear

此引用 chunk 未建立该原子断言的完整技术范围；不给部分支持分。

Uncertainty: 保存片段未建立完整范围；不给部分支持分。

Parent report sentence: pgvector hybrid search is described as useful for enterprise search, document retrieval in knowledge bases, and RAG because it can filter by structured attributes such as department, author, date, or document type while using vector embeddings for semantic ranking\.

Evidence: ev_62fa0fb7bbfd9bb8 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
by expanding what can be achieved using PostgreSQL alone.
What Is Hybrid Search?
Hybrid search combines vector-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering to deliver highly relevant results. While vector search leverages embeddings to find items that are semantically similar, it often lacks precision when users need to constrain their results using structured attributes or keywords.
Hybrid search addresses this gap by enabling both types of queries in a single operation, providing a more refined and context-aware retrieval experience. In this model, a typical query might first filter a dataset based on categorical information or keywords, then apply vector similarity to rank or further narrow the results.
```

## EIV2_TC_001-citation_support-044

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: citation_support; flags: CITATION_SUPPORT_UNCERTAIN

Exact report/unit text:

RAG

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: unclear

此引用 chunk 未建立该原子断言的完整技术范围；不给部分支持分。

Uncertainty: 保存片段未建立完整范围；不给部分支持分。

Parent report sentence: pgvector hybrid search is described as useful for enterprise search, document retrieval in knowledge bases, and RAG because it can filter by structured attributes such as department, author, date, or document type while using vector embeddings for semantic ranking\.

Evidence: ev_a68505e19318d340 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
Document Retrieval in Knowledge Bases
In large knowledge bases or documentation portals, finding the right piece of information can be challenging due to varied terminology, document formats, or organizational silos. Hybrid search with pgvector enables users to filter by document type, author, or date, while also harnessing vector embeddings to find content semantically related to a user’s query. This approach bridges gaps between keywords and actual user intent.
For enterprises, this capability reduces time spent searching and improves knowledge discovery. Team members can locate conceptually related guides, policies, or troubleshooting steps without needing to know exact phrases.
RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation)
```

## EIV2_TC_001-citation_support-045

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: citation_support; flags: CITATION_SUPPORT_UNCERTAIN

Exact report/unit text:

RAG

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: unclear

此引用 chunk 未建立该原子断言的完整技术范围；不给部分支持分。

Uncertainty: 保存片段未建立完整范围；不给部分支持分。

Parent report sentence: pgvector hybrid search is described as useful for enterprise search, document retrieval in knowledge bases, and RAG because it can filter by structured attributes such as department, author, date, or document type while using vector embeddings for semantic ranking\.

Evidence: ev_c87ab4cb76d42dd2 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
Enterprise Search Applications
Organizations often amass vast volumes of structured and unstructured data (emails, reports, contracts, etc.). Hybrid search with pgvector equips internal search engines to surface relevant results across heterogeneous document types by marrying SQL-based filtering (department, author, date) with vector-driven semantic ranking. This improves the search experience for employees, ensuring they retrieve not only exact matches but also contextually relevant documents.
By keeping metadata and embeddings together in PostgreSQL, enterprises maintain robust governance and simplified access control. IT teams can leverage familiar backup, monitoring, and disaster recovery workflows while building advanced search capabilities. The result is more efficient information retrieval, improved productivity, and secure knowledge management.
Related content: Read our guide to Pgvector performance (coming soon)
Tips from the expert
Perry Clark
```

The PostgreSQL query planner can use pgvector indexes for nearest\-neighbor queries

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: support

Parent report sentence: The PostgreSQL query planner can use pgvector indexes for nearest\-neighbor queries, and vector search can be combined with standard SQL filters, joins, and CTEs in the same query\.

Evidence: ev_9c01b4455145f8f5 — https://www.tigerdata.com/blog/the-postgres-developers-guide-to-vector-index-tradeoffs

```text
An HNSW index looks like this:
CREATE INDEX ON documents
USING hnsw (embedding vector_cosine_ops);
For IVFFlat, you define the number of lists and tune the number of probes:
CREATE INDEX ON documents
USING ivfflat (embedding vector_cosine_ops)
WITH (lists = 1000);
SET ivfflat.probes = 10;
The query planner can use these indexes for nearest-neighbor queries, and you can combine vector search with standard SQL filters, joins and CTEs in the same query. For many teams already running Postgres, this can remove the need to operate a separate vector database.
pgvector
can start to show limits at larger scale, especially with high-dimensional embeddings at tens of millions of rows and indexes that no longer fit comfortably in memory. That is the problem
pgvectorscale
was built to address.
pgvectorscale
The DiskANN section above describes a specific problem: vector workloads that have grown too large to keep the working index in memory. For Postgres,
pgvectorscale
```

Vector columns increase backup sizes significantly

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: support

保存片段直接包含此断言的对应机制/用途；不把文中其他未引用断言补入支持。

Parent report sentence: Vector columns increase backup sizes significantly, so teams may need incremental backup strategies, separate tablespaces for vector data, and restore\-time testing with realistic data volumes\.

Evidence: ev_24320d0559472fcb — https://www.velodb.io/glossary/what-is-pgvector

```text
Monitoring
Track these metrics for vector workloads:
Index size and build time
Query latency percentiles (p50, p95, p99)
Recall rate (requires ground truth comparison)
Connection pool utilization during vector queries
Backup Considerations
Vector columns increase backup sizes significantly. Consider:
Incremental backup strategies
Separate tablespaces for vector data
Testing restore times with realistic data volumes
Frequently Asked Questions About pgvector
Do I need a separate database to use pgvector?
No. pgvector is an extension that installs into your existing PostgreSQL database. Enable it with
CREATE EXTENSION vector;
and you can immediately create vector columns in any table. Your vectors and relational data share the same database instance, transactions, and backup procedures.
Is pgvector a vector database?
```

## EIV2_TC_001-citation_support-068

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: citation_support; flags:

Exact report/unit text:

pgvector search quality depends entirely on the upstream embedding model

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: support

保存片段直接包含此断言的对应机制/用途；不把文中其他未引用断言补入支持。

Uncertainty: 文本支持建议仍待来源日期和人工确认。

Parent report sentence: pgvector search quality depends entirely on the upstream embedding model; the extension stores and retrieves vectors but does not understand or improve semantic quality, so poor embeddings produce poor results regardless of database performance\.

Evidence: ev_648babff2ebe3058 — https://www.velodb.io/glossary/what-is-pgvector

```text
Embedding Model Dependency
Search quality depends entirely on your upstream embedding model. pgvector stores and retrieves vectors—it doesn't understand or improve the semantic quality of your embeddings. Poor embeddings produce poor results regardless of database performance.
Operational Complexity at Scale
Managing pgvector in production requires PostgreSQL expertise:
Vacuum operations become more complex with large vector tables
Index maintenance requires downtime or careful online rebuilding
Monitoring vector-specific performance requires custom instrumentation
Backup and restore times increase significantly with vector data
When Should You Choose pgvector?
pgvector fits specific scenarios well. Consider it when:
You already run PostgreSQL
: The extension adds vector capabilities without new infrastructure, operational complexity, or vendor relationships.
Vector search is a feature, not the core product
```

reviewer_identity: null; human_decision: null; review_timestamp: null; adjudication_note: null

## EIV2_TC_001-required_unit-001

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: required_unit; flags: REQUIRED_UNIT_REVIEW

Exact report/unit text:

Hybrid search in pgvector contexts combines vector\-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering because vector search handles semantic meaning but can fail on specific keywords or structured constraints\.

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: NOT_SATISFIED

机制有明确描述，但当前引用未建立 pgvector 项目的一手材料加独立佐证组合；不能用多个教程自动满足 primary_plus_independent。

Uncertainty: 严格完整覆盖及证据强度需人工裁定；不计部分分。

Frozen Required Unit: 混合检索机制

Evidence: ev_62fa0fb7bbfd9bb8 — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
by expanding what can be achieved using PostgreSQL alone.
What Is Hybrid Search?
Hybrid search combines vector-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering to deliver highly relevant results. While vector search leverages embeddings to find items that are semantically similar, it often lacks precision when users need to constrain their results using structured attributes or keywords.
Hybrid search addresses this gap by enabling both types of queries in a single operation, providing a more refined and context-aware retrieval experience. In this model, a typical query might first filter a dataset based on categorical information or keywords, then apply vector similarity to rank or further narrow the results.
```

Evidence: ev_7a6809120ded0e6a — https://www.instaclustr.com/education/vector-database/pgvector-hybrid-search-benefits-use-cases-and-quick-tutorial

```text
This approach is valuable when dealing with real-world data, where both the semantic meaning and structured attributes are important. Tools like pgvector make hybrid search feasible within PostgreSQL, allowing developers to combine modern AI-powered methods with classic SQL querying.
This is part of a series of articles about
vector database
Benefits of Using Pgvector for Hybrid Search
Using
pgvector
for hybrid search in PostgreSQL environments offers several practical advantages:
Single-platform integration
: pgvector allows teams to manage both structured data and vector embeddings in the same PostgreSQL instance. This eliminates the need for separate vector databases or synchronization layers.
Simplified query logic
: Hybrid search queries can be expressed using standard SQL syntax, combining traditional filters (WHERE, JOIN, etc.) with vector similarity functions (<->). This makes complex queries more maintainable and easier to debug.
Reduced latency
```

Evidence: ev_4426f461ab217c20 — https://medium.com/@aysebilgegunduz/all-you-need-to-know-about-pgvector-part-4-hybrid-search-8a655d3b0087

```text
blog series. In this post I’ll be talking about Hybrid Search that is a combination of text and vector search. For previous parts of this blog series, I covered

where you could combine filters with vector search,
Indexing
where I talked about why and how to do indexing for vector data in PostgreSQL and the finally the
```

```text
The DiskANN section above describes a specific problem: vector workloads that have grown too large to keep the working index in memory. For Postgres,
pgvectorscale
```

Evidence: ev_e38e6e3a23869cb1 — https://www.velodb.io/glossary/what-is-pgvector

```text
Query Patterns and Performance Characteristics
pgvector queries integrate naturally with SQL, enabling powerful hybrid searches:
SELECT
d.content, d.embedding
<=>
query_embedding
AS
similarity
FROM
documents d
JOIN
categories c
ON
d.category_id
=
c.id
WHERE
c.name
=
'technology'
AND
d.created_at
>
'2024-01-01'
ORDER
BY
similarity
LIMIT
20
;
This combines vector similarity with relational filters and joins—something that requires complex orchestration with standalone vector databases.
Performance scales with dataset size, vector dimensions, and index configuration. For datasets under 1 million vectors with proper indexing, pgvector typically delivers sub-second query latency.
Why Developers Use pgvector
pgvector has gained significant adoption for several practical reasons:
Unified Infrastructure
No need to deploy, manage, or pay for a separate vector database. Your vectors live alongside application data in the same PostgreSQL instance you already operate.
Familiar SQL Interface
```

## EIV2_TC_001-required_unit-003

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: required_unit; flags: REQUIRED_UNIT_REVIEW

Exact report/unit text:

Managing pgvector in production requires PostgreSQL expertise: vacuum operations become more complex with large vector tables, index maintenance requires downtime or careful online rebuilding, monitoring vector\-specific performance requires custom instrumentation, and backup and restore times increase significantly with vector data\.

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: UNCERTAIN

报告有多项限制但具体断言主要来自同一 VeloDB 页面，ParadeDB 是另一厂商文章；需要逐断言确认独立来源、利益关系和性能风险所需证据。

Uncertainty: 严格完整覆盖及证据强度需人工裁定；不计部分分。

Frozen Required Unit: 企业工作负载限制

Evidence: ev_648babff2ebe3058 — https://www.velodb.io/glossary/what-is-pgvector

```text
Embedding Model Dependency
Search quality depends entirely on your upstream embedding model. pgvector stores and retrieves vectors—it doesn't understand or improve the semantic quality of your embeddings. Poor embeddings produce poor results regardless of database performance.
Operational Complexity at Scale
Managing pgvector in production requires PostgreSQL expertise:
Vacuum operations become more complex with large vector tables
Index maintenance requires downtime or careful online rebuilding
Monitoring vector-specific performance requires custom instrumentation
Backup and restore times increase significantly with vector data
When Should You Choose pgvector?
pgvector fits specific scenarios well. Consider it when:
You already run PostgreSQL
: The extension adds vector capabilities without new infrastructure, operational complexity, or vendor relationships.
Vector search is a feature, not the core product
```

Evidence: ev_24320d0559472fcb — https://www.velodb.io/glossary/what-is-pgvector

```text
Monitoring
Track these metrics for vector workloads:
Index size and build time
Query latency percentiles (p50, p95, p99)
Recall rate (requires ground truth comparison)
Connection pool utilization during vector queries
Backup Considerations
Vector columns increase backup sizes significantly. Consider:
Incremental backup strategies
Separate tablespaces for vector data
Testing restore times with realistic data volumes
Frequently Asked Questions About pgvector
Do I need a separate database to use pgvector?
No. pgvector is an extension that installs into your existing PostgreSQL database. Enable it with
CREATE EXTENSION vector;
and you can immediately create vector columns in any table. Your vectors and relational data share the same database instance, transactions, and backup procedures.
Is pgvector a vector database?
```

Evidence: ev_4b2cc9add6171347 — https://www.paradedb.com/learn/postgresql/pgvector-limitations

```text
Summary
pgvector is the right choice when your dataset fits comfortably on one PostgreSQL instance, your filter selectivity is moderate, and you value transactional consistency with the rest of your relational data. The edges to watch are index build cost, filtered query performance, missing hybrid and reranking primitives, and the absence of horizontal scaling. Knowing where those edges sit makes it easier to decide whether pgvector is sufficient on its own, whether to pair it with
full-text search
for
hybrid retrieval
, or whether part of the workload belongs in a dedicated engine.
Previous
Next
```

```text
by expanding what can be achieved using PostgreSQL alone.
What Is Hybrid Search?
Hybrid search combines vector-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering to deliver highly relevant results. While vector search leverages embeddings to find items that are semantically similar, it often lacks precision when users need to constrain their results using structured attributes or keywords.
Hybrid search addresses this gap by enabling both types of queries in a single operation, providing a more refined and context-aware retrieval experience. In this model, a typical query might first filter a dataset based on categorical information or keywords, then apply vector similarity to rank or further narrow the results.
```

```text
Benefits of Using Pgvector for Hybrid Search
Using
pgvector
for hybrid search in PostgreSQL environments offers several practical advantages:
Single-platform integration
: pgvector allows teams to manage both structured data and vector embeddings in the same PostgreSQL instance. This eliminates the need for separate vector databases or synchronization layers.
Simplified query logic
: Hybrid search queries can be expressed using standard SQL syntax, combining traditional filters (WHERE, JOIN, etc.) with vector similarity functions (<->). This makes complex queries more maintainable and easier to debug.
Reduced latency
```

## EIV2_TC_001-evidence_strength-009

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: evidence_strength; flags: EVIDENCE_STRENGTH_REVIEW

Exact report/unit text:

Tiger Data vector index tradeoffs

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: NOT_PRIMARY_FOR_PGVECTOR

Tiger Data 文章提供 SQL 示例并介绍 pgvectorscale；不据此认定所有 pgvector 性能结论为项目一手证明。

Uncertainty: 一手性只限此片段可证实的来源角色，不等于真实性或 authority 分数。

Evidence: ev_9c01b4455145f8f5 — https://www.tigerdata.com/blog/the-postgres-developers-guide-to-vector-index-tradeoffs

```text
An HNSW index looks like this:
CREATE INDEX ON documents
USING hnsw (embedding vector_cosine_ops);
For IVFFlat, you define the number of lists and tune the number of probes:
CREATE INDEX ON documents
USING ivfflat (embedding vector_cosine_ops)
WITH (lists = 1000);
SET ivfflat.probes = 10;
The query planner can use these indexes for nearest-neighbor queries, and you can combine vector search with standard SQL filters, joins and CTEs in the same query. For many teams already running Postgres, this can remove the need to operate a separate vector database.
pgvector
can start to show limits at larger scale, especially with high-dimensional embeddings at tens of millions of rows and indexes that no longer fit comfortably in memory. That is the problem
pgvectorscale
was built to address.
pgvectorscale
The DiskANN section above describes a specific problem: vector workloads that have grown too large to keep the working index in memory. For Postgres,
pgvectorscale
```

```text
Document Retrieval in Knowledge Bases
In large knowledge bases or documentation portals, finding the right piece of information can be challenging due to varied terminology, document formats, or organizational silos. Hybrid search with pgvector enables users to filter by document type, author, or date, while also harnessing vector embeddings to find content semantically related to a user’s query. This approach bridges gaps between keywords and actual user intent.
For enterprises, this capability reduces time spent searching and improves knowledge discovery. Team members can locate conceptually related guides, policies, or troubleshooting steps without needing to know exact phrases.
RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation)
```

```text
Enterprise Search Applications
Organizations often amass vast volumes of structured and unstructured data (emails, reports, contracts, etc.). Hybrid search with pgvector equips internal search engines to surface relevant results across heterogeneous document types by marrying SQL-based filtering (department, author, date) with vector-driven semantic ranking. This improves the search experience for employees, ensuring they retrieve not only exact matches but also contextually relevant documents.
By keeping metadata and embeddings together in PostgreSQL, enterprises maintain robust governance and simplified access control. IT teams can leverage familiar backup, monitoring, and disaster recovery workflows while building advanced search capabilities. The result is more efficient information retrieval, improved productivity, and secure knowledge management.
```

## EIV2_TC_001-independence-005

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: independence; flags:

Exact report/unit text:

ParadeDB pgvector Limitations

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: SAME_SOURCE_GROUP

使用内容身份与来源角色建议分组；同一页面的多个 chunk 不重复计为独立来源。不同域名本身不证明独立性。标题与文中视角表明第三方产品厂商介绍 pgvector 限制；未展示原始测量，不以 authority prior 代替一手性。

Evidence: ev_4b2cc9add6171347 — https://www.paradedb.com/learn/postgresql/pgvector-limitations

```text
Summary
pgvector is the right choice when your dataset fits comfortably on one PostgreSQL instance, your filter selectivity is moderate, and you value transactional consistency with the rest of your relational data. The edges to watch are index build cost, filtered query performance, missing hybrid and reranking primitives, and the absence of horizontal scaling. Knowing where those edges sit makes it easier to decide whether pgvector is sufficient on its own, whether to pair it with
full-text search
for
hybrid retrieval
, or whether part of the workload belongs in a dedicated engine.
Previous
Next
```

```text
by expanding what can be achieved using PostgreSQL alone.
What Is Hybrid Search?
Hybrid search combines vector-based similarity search with traditional keyword or metadata filtering to deliver highly relevant results. While vector search leverages embeddings to find items that are semantically similar, it often lacks precision when users need to constrain their results using structured attributes or keywords.
Hybrid search addresses this gap by enabling both types of queries in a single operation, providing a more refined and context-aware retrieval experience. In this model, a typical query might first filter a dataset based on categorical information or keywords, then apply vector similarity to rank or further narrow the results.
```

## EIV2_TC_001-independence-007

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: independence; flags:

Exact report/unit text:

VeloDB 的 pgvector 解释文章

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: SAME_SOURCE_GROUP

使用内容身份与来源角色建议分组；同一页面的多个 chunk 不重复计为独立来源。不同域名本身不证明独立性。保存页面是 VeloDB 介绍 pgvector，未展示原始性能测量或 pgvector 项目作者身份。

Uncertainty: 组内去重可确认，跨组独立性仍需人工审核。

Evidence: ev_648babff2ebe3058 — https://www.velodb.io/glossary/what-is-pgvector

```text
Embedding Model Dependency
Search quality depends entirely on your upstream embedding model. pgvector stores and retrieves vectors—it doesn't understand or improve the semantic quality of your embeddings. Poor embeddings produce poor results regardless of database performance.
Operational Complexity at Scale
Managing pgvector in production requires PostgreSQL expertise:
Vacuum operations become more complex with large vector tables
Index maintenance requires downtime or careful online rebuilding
Monitoring vector-specific performance requires custom instrumentation
Backup and restore times increase significantly with vector data
When Should You Choose pgvector?
pgvector fits specific scenarios well. Consider it when:
You already run PostgreSQL
: The extension adds vector capabilities without new infrastructure, operational complexity, or vendor relationships.
Vector search is a feature, not the core product
```

```text
This approach is valuable when dealing with real-world data, where both the semantic meaning and structured attributes are important. Tools like pgvector make hybrid search feasible within PostgreSQL, allowing developers to combine modern AI-powered methods with classic SQL querying.
This is part of a series of articles about
vector database
Benefits of Using Pgvector for Hybrid Search
Using
pgvector
for hybrid search in PostgreSQL environments offers several practical advantages:
Single-platform integration
: pgvector allows teams to manage both structured data and vector embeddings in the same PostgreSQL instance. This eliminates the need for separate vector databases or synchronization layers.
Simplified query logic
: Hybrid search queries can be expressed using standard SQL syntax, combining traditional filters (WHERE, JOIN, etc.) with vector similarity functions (<->). This makes complex queries more maintainable and easier to debug.
Reduced latency
```

## EIV2_TC_001-independence-009

Case/category: EIV2_TC_001 / technical_capability_analysis

Type: independence; flags:

Exact report/unit text:

Tiger Data vector index tradeoffs

AI_ASSISTED_RECOMMENDATION: SAME_SOURCE_GROUP

使用内容身份与来源角色建议分组；同一页面的多个 chunk 不重复计为独立来源。不同域名本身不证明独立性。Tiger Data 文章提供 SQL 示例并介绍 pgvectorscale；不据此认定所有 pgvector 性能结论为项目一手证明。

Uncertainty: 组内去重可确认，跨组独立性仍需人工审核。

Evidence: ev_9c01b4455145f8f5 — https://www.tigerdata.com/blog/the-postgres-developers-guide-to-vector-index-tradeoffs

```text
An HNSW index looks like this:
CREATE INDEX ON documents
USING hnsw (embedding vector_cosine_ops);
For IVFFlat, you define the number of lists and tune the number of probes:
CREATE INDEX ON documents
USING ivfflat (embedding vector_cosine_ops)
WITH (lists = 1000);
SET ivfflat.probes = 10;
The query planner can use these indexes for nearest-neighbor queries, and you can combine vector search with standard SQL filters, joins and CTEs in the same query. For many teams already running Postgres, this can remove the need to operate a separate vector database.
pgvector
can start to show limits at larger scale, especially with high-dimensional embeddings at tens of millions of rows and indexes that no longer fit comfortably in memory. That is the problem
pgvectorscale
was built to address.
pgvectorscale
The DiskANN section above describes a specific problem: vector workloads that have grown too large to keep the working index in memory. For Postgres,
pgvectorscale
```

```text
Document Retrieval in Knowledge Bases
In large knowledge bases or documentation portals, finding the right piece of information can be challenging due to varied terminology, document formats, or organizational silos. Hybrid search with pgvector enables users to filter by document type, author, or date, while also harnessing vector embeddings to find content semantically related to a user’s query. This approach bridges gaps between keywords and actual user intent.
For enterprises, this capability reduces time spent searching and improves knowledge discovery. Team members can locate conceptually related guides, policies, or troubleshooting steps without needing to know exact phrases.
RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation)
```
